## Sheet: `Instructions`

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
|  | Instructions |  |
|  |  |  |
|  | Field Name | Instructions/Descriptions |
|  | Field Group | Mercans' field group name. The values in this field should not be updated. |
|  | Field Name | Data field name in Mercans' system. This field should be updated only for the field in the Implementation-Specific Fields tab. The values in this field are not always the same as the actual database field names. |
|  | Internal Code | Mercans' data field code. This field is used to map Client's data against Mercans fields. |
|  | Label in Local Language | Field name in the local language and should reflect the same terminology as used on statutory forms or local language reports. |
|  | Field Description (Long Description of the Field) | Include a full description of the respective data field. |
|  | Data Type - Mandatory (M), Conditional Mandatory (CM), Optional (O) | The values of this field should not be updated for Universal or Country-Specific Fields. Each Implementation-Specific Fields must be categorized as a either Mandatory, Conditional Mandatory or Optional fields. Mandatory fields must be filled for each employee record and Conditional Mandatory fields only if a certain condition is met. |
|  | Pre-Condition for Conditional Mandatory | This field should be filled for all data fields identified as Conditional-Minatory. It should include the condition that makes the respective field mandatory. |
|  | Field Type (Text, Number, Date, Integer, Decimal, Counter) | Available field type options are Text, Number, Date, Integer, Decimal, and Counter. Each field must include a field type. |
|  | Pre-Defined Format | Field format should be provided for the fields that must be in specific formats; i.e. (999) 123-4567, etc. |
|  | Validations, Restrictions, Pick Lists etc. | Field-specific validations are defined in this field. Validations can include specific pick lists, length validations, etc. |
|  | Default Value | A default value should be filled for each record if no other value is applicable. |
|  | MSS Field Permissions | For each field identify whether the field is not visible/available (hidden), visible but not editable (view) or editable (edit) through Payroll Administrator Console (PAC). Further field specific access restrictions can be defined based on PAC user roles and access rights. |
|  | ESS Field Permissions | For each field identify whether the field is not visible/available (hidden), visible but not editable (view) or editable (edit) through Employee Self Service (ESS) by employees for their own records. |
|  | Additional Notes | Include any additional clarifications or notes here. |

## Sheet: `ID Information`

| Identification Information |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Country | Ordinal | Field Name | Internal Code | Label in Local Language | Field Description (Long Description of the Field) | Data Type  -Mandatory (M), Conditional Mandatory (CM), Optional (O) | Pre-Condition for Conditional Mandatory | Field Type (Text, Number, Date, Integer, Decimal, Counter) | Pre-Defined Format | Option Mapper | Validations, Restrictions, Pick Lists etc. | Parent Field Group | Ordinal of Parent Field Group | Internal Code of Parent Field Group | Sub Group under Parent Field Group | Ordinal of Sub Group under Parent Field Group | Internal Code of Sub Group under Parent Field Group | Default Value | MSS Field Permissions (Read, Write) | ESS Field Permissions (Read, Write) | Additional Notes |
| Belgium |  | National Register Number (INSZ / NISS) |  | Rijksregisternummer (INSZ / NISS) | The single 11-digit number used for both tax and social security purposes. Positions 1-6 are the date of birth (YYMMDD), positions 7-9 a sequence number that is odd for men and even for women, and positions 10-11 the check digits computed as 97 minus the first nine digits modulo 97. For persons born from 1 January 2000 a '2' is prefixed to the first nine digits before the modulo is taken. It is the key of the whole Belgian social security chain. | M |  | Text | YY.MM.DD-SSS.CC (11 digits) | Identification Number Type |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Mandatory on the Dimona, the DmfA, every DRS scenario, the FEDRIS work accident declaration, the Limosa declaration and the 281.10 certificate. An employee cannot be declared without it. Sources: BE-NSSO-001 zone 00024; BE-NSSO-002 block 90017; BE-FIN-002 zone 2.019; CCG Module 2.2. |
| Belgium |  | BIS Number |  | Bisnummer | Identification number with the same 11-digit structure as the INSZ, issued by the Crossroads Bank for Social Security to persons who are not entered in the National Register, typically short-term foreign workers. The birth month is increased by 20 where the gender was unknown at the time of allocation, or by 40 where it was known. | CM | The employee is not entered in the Belgian National Register. | Text | YY.MM.DD-SSS.CC (11 digits) | Identification Number Type |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | The BIS number is used in every place the INSZ is used. It must be obtained before the first pay run. Source: CCG Module 2.2; BE-NSSO-005 field LIM-024. |
| Belgium |  | Identification Number Type |  | Type identificatienummer | Selects whether the number held for the employee is a National Register number or a BIS number. It drives the validation routine applied to the check digits and the reporting behaviour where the person is not on the National Register. | M |  | Text |  | Identification Number Type |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Configuration selector. Source: CCG Module 2.2. |
| Belgium |  | Tax Identification Number (FIN) |  | Fiscaal identificatienummer (FIN) | The foreign tax identification number of the recipient of the income, reported in zone 2.030 of the 281.10 fiche where the recipient has no Belgian national number or where the foreign number is additionally required. | CM | The employee is a non-resident, or holds a foreign tax identification number that the 281.10 requires. | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-FIN-002 zone 2.030. Source: BE-FIN-002 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | SIS Card Number |  | SIS-kaartnummer | The number of the social identity card. The SIS card was withdrawn and the zone is no longer populated on new declarations; the field is retained only so that historical declarations and corrections covering periods in which the card was in use can be reproduced. | O |  | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Present in the NSSO glossary for blocks 90017 of the Dimona, the DmfA and every DRS scenario, where it is left unpopulated. Do not collect for new hires. Source: BE-NSSO-001 zone 00025; BE-NSSO-002 block 90017. |
| Belgium |  | Employee Payroll Reference |  | Werknemersreferentie payroll | The employer's own reference for the employee, carried through the declarations as a free correlation key so that acknowledgements, anomaly reports and the 325.10 summary can be matched back to the payroll record. | O |  | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Maps to the Natural Person User Reference of the NSSO blocks and to zone 2.018 Recipient Reference of the 281.10. Never used by the authority for identification. Source: BE-NSSO-001 zone 00110 area; BE-FIN-002 zone 2.018. |
| Belgium |  | Health Insurance Fund Code |  | Code van het ziekenfonds (mutualiteit) | The code of the health insurance fund (mutualiteit / mutualite) with which the employee is affiliated. Required on the FEDRIS work accident declaration so that the fund can be identified for the benefit chain. | CM | A work accident or an occupational disease declaration is filed for the employee. | Text |  | Health Insurance Fund Code |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-FEDRIS-001 block 90073. The code list is not held in 00-official-sources; see the Country Option Mappers sheet. Source: BE-FEDRIS-001 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Health Insurance Fund Affiliation Number |  | Aansluitingsnummer bij het ziekenfonds | The employee's membership number with the health insurance fund, reported alongside the fund code on the work accident declaration. | CM | A work accident or an occupational disease declaration is filed for the employee. | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-FEDRIS-001 block 90073 Mutuality Affiliation Id. Source: BE-FEDRIS-001 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Health Insurance Fund Name |  | Naam van het ziekenfonds (mutualiteit) | The name of the health insurance fund with which the employee is affiliated, reported alongside the fund code on the FEDRIS work accident declaration. It must be captured explicitly because the register of fund codes is not held, so the name cannot be derived from the code. | CM | A work accident or an occupational disease declaration is filed for the employee. | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-FEDRIS-001 block 90073 Mutuality Name. Source: BE-FEDRIS-001 Sheet 4. |

| Country | Ordinal | Field Name | Internal Code | Label in Local Language | Field Description (Long Description of the Field) | Data Type  -Mandatory (M), Conditional Mandatory (CM), Optional (O) | Pre-Condition for Conditional Mandatory | Field Type (Text, Number, Date, Integer, Decimal, Counter) | Pre-Defined Format | Option Mapper | Validations, Restrictions, Pick Lists etc. | Parent Field Group | Ordinal of Parent Field Group | Internal Code of Parent Field Group | Sub Group under Parent Field Group | Ordinal of Sub Group under Parent Field Group | Internal Code of Sub Group under Parent Field Group | Default Value | MSS Field Permissions (Read, Write) | ESS Field Permissions (Read, Write) | Additional Notes |
| Belgium |  | Enterprise Number (KBO / BCE) |  | Ondernemingsnummer (KBO / BCE) | The number that establishes the legal existence of the enterprise in the Crossroads Bank for Enterprises. Ten digits of which the first is 0 or 1 and the last two are a modulo-97 check on the preceding eight. It is allotted centrally at federal level and does not vary by region. | M |  | Text | 0nnn.nnn.nnn (10 digits) |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Required before any activity and used on the Dimona, the DmfA, the 281.10, the Limosa declaration and the FEDRIS declaration. Source: CCG Module 2.2; Law of 16 January 2003. |
| Belgium |  | Establishment Unit Number |  | Vestigingseenheidsnummer | The number granted per physical place of activity. Ten digits beginning with 2. It carries the address that determines which language regime applies to personnel documents and the region that drives child benefit, training incentives and regional target-group reductions. | M |  | Text | 2nnn.nnn.nnn (10 digits) |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | One per physical place of activity. The region of the establishment unit, not of the employee's home, drives the regional entitlements. Source: CCG Module 2.2. |
| Belgium |  | VAT Number |  | BTW-nummer | The VAT identification number, formed by prefixing BE to the ten-digit enterprise number. The body of the number is identical to the enterprise number. | M |  | Text | BEnnnnnnnnnn |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Required before taxable activity. Source: CCG Module 2.2. |
| Belgium |  | NSSO Employer Registration Number |  | RSZ-werkgeversnummer | The employer identification number of the National Social Security Office, composed of an 8-digit order number (positions 1-8, first digit 0 or 1) and a 2-digit modulo-97 check (positions 9-10): check = 97 - (order_number MOD 97); if the remainder is 0, check = 97 (same convention as the Belgian national number and the OGM/VCS structured communication). Nine or twelve positions; some provisional NSSO numbers use 10 digits. Verified 21 Aug 2026 against the DmfA glossary field definition (structure) and cross-checked against the Belgian mod-97 identifier family (NISS, OGM-VCS) for the arithmetic, since NSSO does not publish the raw formula in a single public document. RECOMMENDATION: unit-test against >=10 known real NSSO numbers before relying on this for validation - confirm which digits form the base number if any real number fails (a 3-digit category-index-prefixed presentation may need normalising to the 8-digit order form first). | M |  | Text | 9 or 12 positions |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Allotted automatically on the first Dimona declaration. Required on the Dimona, the DmfA, every DRS scenario and the FEDRIS declaration. Source: CCG Module 2.2; BE-NSSO-001 zone 00014; BE-NSSO-002 block 90007. |
| Belgium |  | Provisional NSSO Number |  | Voorlopig RSZ-nummer | The provisional identification number used in place of the definitive NSSO employer number until the definitive number is allocated. | CM | A definitive NSSO employer registration number has not yet been allotted. | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Only ever populated instead of, never alongside, the definitive number. Source: BE-NSSO-001 zone 00015. |
| Belgium |  | NSSO Employer Category |  | RSZ-werkgeverscategorie | The three-digit category allotted by the NSSO per employer. It selects the employer's contribution obligations and therefore which contribution and reduction codes may appear on the DmfA. | M |  | Text | nnn (3 digits) | NSSO Employer Category |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Taken from the NSSO registration confirmation; it is client-specific and cannot be derived. Source: BE-NSSO-002 zone 00036. |
| Belgium |  | Sub-Entity Number |  | Subentiteitsnummer | The identification number of a sub-entity of the employer, used where the NSSO has granted sub-entities under a single employer registration. | CM | The employer has been granted one or more NSSO sub-entities. | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-NSSO-001 block 90374 Sub Entity Nbr. Source: BE-NSSO-001 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Local Unit Identification Number |  | Identificatienummer van de lokale eenheid | The local unit identification number of the place where the work is performed, used on the DmfA worker record, the occupation line and the student contribution block to attribute employment to a site. | CM | The employer operates more than one local unit. | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-NSSO-002 blocks 90012, 90015 and 90003 and BE-FEDRIS-001 block 90056. Source: BE-NSSO-002 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Wage Withholding Tax Registration Number |  | Registratienummer bedrijfsvoorheffing | The reference number under which the employer is registered as a withholding agent with FPS Finance. The enterprise number is used, and access to Finprof is granted per enterprise number. | M |  | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Registration is required before the first wage payment and gives access to Finprof and Belcotax-on-web. Drives BE-FIN-001 Employer Reference Number and BE-FIN-002 zone 2.014. Source: CCG Module 2.2; BE-FIN-001 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Tax Collection District Code |  | Districtcode van het ontvangkantoor | The code of the district of the collection office to which the employer reports its wage withholding tax. It is allotted in the register of jurisdictions of FPS Finance and is client-specific. | M |  | Text |  | Tax Collection District and Office |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives the Receipt block of BE-FIN-001. Not derivable from any other field. Source: BE-FIN-001 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Tax Collection Office Code |  | Kantoorcode van het ontvangkantoor | The code of the collection office itself, reported together with the district code on the wage withholding tax return. | M |  | Text |  | Tax Collection District and Office |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives the Receipt block of BE-FIN-001. Source: BE-FIN-001 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Joint Committee Number |  | Paritair comitenummer | The number of the joint committee (paritair comite) competent for the employer's main activity. It determines the applicable sectoral collective bargaining agreements, the minimum wage scales, the end-of-year premium and the sectoral funds collected through the DmfA. Three to six digits, left-aligned, structured as CCC, CCC.CC or CCC.CC.CC. | M |  | Text | CCC, CCC.CC or CCC.CC.CC | Joint Committee Number |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Determined by the main activity and confirmed with the FPS Employment. A change of joint committee opens a new occupation line on the DmfA. Source: CCG Module 2.2; BE-NSSO-002 zone 00046. |
| Belgium |  | NACE Activity Code |  | NACE-activiteitscode | The code of the economic activity of the employer, declared on the DmfA occupation line and on the complementary indemnity block. | M |  | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-NSSO-002 Activity Code. Source: BE-NSSO-002 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Work Accident Insurer Identification |  | Identificatie van de arbeidsongevallenverzekeraar | The identification of the private insurer with which the compulsory insurance against occupational accidents is placed. The insurance must be in place before the first employee starts work. | M |  | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-FEDRIS-001 block 90027 Insurer Id. Source: CCG Module 2.2; Law of 4 August 1996; BE-FEDRIS-001 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Work Accident Policy Number |  | Polisnummer arbeidsongevallenverzekering | The number of the work accident insurance policy, reported on the FEDRIS declaration so that the insurer can attach the claim to the correct contract. | M |  | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-FEDRIS-001 block 90027 Policy Identifier. Source: BE-FEDRIS-001 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | Work Accident Policy Subdivision Number |  | Onderverdelingsnummer van de polis | The subdivision of the work accident policy, used where the insurer has split the policy by site, activity or personnel category. | O |  | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-FEDRIS-001 block 90027 Subdivision Number. Source: BE-FEDRIS-001 Sheet 4. |
| Belgium |  | External Prevention Service Enterprise Number |  | Ondernemingsnummer van de externe dienst voor preventie en bescherming | The enterprise number of the external service for prevention and protection at work to which the employer is affiliated. Affiliation to a compulsory occupational health service is required before the first employee starts work. | M |  | Text | 0nnn.nnn.nnn (10 digits) |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Drives BE-NSSO-004 block 90564 Prevention Service. Source: CCG Module 2.2; Law of 4 August 1996; BE-NSSO-004 Sheet 4. |

|  | Belgium |  | Foreign Postal Code |  | Buitenlandse postcode | The postal code of a non-Belgian address, reported in zone 2.031 of the 281.10 in place of the Belgian postal code zone. | CM | The employee's country of residence is not Belgium. | Text |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | BE-FIN-002 zone 2.031. |
|  | Belgium |  | Language of Personnel Documents |  | Taal van de personeelsdocumenten | The language in which the payslip, the employment contract and the work rules must be drawn up. It is imposed by law according to the region of the employer's seat of operation, not by the employee's residence, the contract language or the parties' preference. In bilingual Brussels-Capital it follows the language of the employee. There are no bilingual personnel documents in Belgium; each document is unilingual. | M |  | Text |  | Language |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Using the wrong language causes nullity in Flanders and Wallonia, translation at the employer's expense with retroactive effect in Brussels, and replacement in the German-language region. F26 / YTD DECISION (2026-08-16, regression-fix pass): this is a take-on balance sourced from the NSSO Student@work counter or the previous provider under scope_manifest.json integration BE-IN-STUDENT-QUOTA, NOT a year-to-date accumulation of a BE-WTC-001 wage type of this employer. It is therefore master data and belongs in the Data Dictionary. Belgium deliberately does NOT carry it through the BE-WTC-001 YTD Load / YTD Remarks mechanism (Wage Type Catalogue columns S and T), which is unpopulated on all 355 coded rows. See 07-qa-crosscheck/DD_REVIEW.md. |

F26 / YTD DECISION (2026-08-16, regression-fix pass): this is a take-on balance sourced from the court, bailiff or administration that served the order under scope_manifest.json integration BE-IN-GARNISHMENT-ORDER, NOT a year-to-date accumulation of a BE-WTC-001 wage type of this employer. It is therefore master data and belongs in the Data Dictionary. Belgium deliberately does NOT carry it through the BE-WTC-001 YTD Load / YTD Remarks mechanism (Wage Type Catalogue columns S and T), which is unpopulated on all 355 coded rows. See 07-qa-crosscheck/DD_REVIEW.md. |

|  | Belgium |  | Months of Service in the Holiday Service Year |  | Maanden dienst in het vakantiedienstjaar | The number of months of service in the holiday service year, loaded at implementation. The double holiday pay of white-collar staff is calculated as 92 per cent of the monthly salary prorated on the months worked in the holiday service year. | CM | The employee is a white-collar employee and the implementation occurs mid-year. | Integer |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | Implementation take-on value. F26 / YTD DECISION (2026-08-17, final round 1 - the fifth take-on field, missed by the 2026-08-16 regression-fix pass that noted the other four): this is a take-on balance sourced from the previous employer's holiday certificate (vakantieattest) under scope_manifest.json integration BE-IN-HOLIDAY-CERT (BE-SIR-001 IN-08), NOT a year-to-date accumulation of a BE-WTC-001 wage type of this employer. The certificate is the source of this specific balance because CCG Module 2.2 records the vakantieattest as the previous-employment document the new employer needs "when paying the double holiday pay", Module 2.3 lists the double holiday pay among the year-to-date-dependent items with the take-on requirement stated as "months of service in the service year, and any holiday pay already paid", and Module 4.2 gives the calculation as 92 per cent of the gross monthly salary x months of service in the holiday service year / 12 - the months of service being what this field holds. It is therefore master data and belongs in the Data Dictionary. Belgium deliberately does NOT carry it through the BE-WTC-001 YTD Load / YTD Remarks mechanism (Wage Type Catalogue columns S and T), which is unpopulated on all 355 coded rows. See 07-qa-crosscheck/DD_REVIEW.md. |

|  |  | 2 | Female | Vrouw | NSSO zone 00029 value for a woman. Positions 7 to 9 of the INSZ are even for women. |
|  |  | X | Other or unspecified | Andere of niet gespecifieerd | Sample value recorded in CCG Module 2.2. NO NSSO CODE EXISTS: zone 00029 admits only 1 and 2, so an X must be resolved to 1 or 2 from the INSZ parity before any NSSO declaration is built. |

|  |  | WORKER | Blue-collar worker | Arbeider | The contribution base is the gross at 108 per cent, funding the holiday fund. Must be paid at least twice per month at intervals of no more than 16 days unless a binding sectoral CBA provides otherwise. |

|  | Option Mapper Description | Code | Abbreviation | Local Name | English Description |
|  | Contract Duration Type | 0 | Indefinite | Onbepaalde duur | FEDRIS / NSSO zone 00885 value 0. |
|  |  | 1 | Fixed term | Bepaalde duur | FEDRIS / NSSO zone 00885 value 1. An end date is mandatory on the Dimona for fixed-term worker types. |
|  |  | n/a | Clearly defined work | Duidelijk omschreven werk | Work for a clearly defined task (duidelijk omschreven werk). RESOLVED - NOT A DmfA REPORTING FIELD, 16 September 2026. The NSSO contract-type zone is 00050 (TYPE DU CONTRAT / type van het contract), and its definition domain is a strict binary: 0 = temps plein / voltijds (full time), 1 = temps partiel / deeltijds (part time). Nothing else has ever been in that domain, across seven independent glossary editions 2005-2021 and again in the DmfA, Dimona and DRS glossaries at edition 2026/2. This variant is a contract-type concept of the Employment Contracts Act of 3 July 1978, not a declarable DmfA value: no zone carries it, so no code can be mapped and none has been invented. It is retained as a Mercans-internal contract-duration value whose NSSO counterpart is whichever of 0 or 1 the actual working time gives. CITATION CORRECTED IN THIS PASS: the earlier text on this row cited 'NSSO zone 00885'. No zone 00885 exists in any held glossary - the contract-type zone is 00050. Source: NSSO DmfA glossary edition 2026/2 (published 01/06/2026), zone 00050 TYPE DU CONTRAT, XML label ContractType, functional block 90015 Occupation. |

|  | Option Mapper Description | Code | Abbreviation | Local Name | English Description |
|  | Worker Status (private sector employment categories) | 1 | Blue-collar worker | Arbeider | Holiday pay through the holiday fund. MERCANS-INTERNAL CODE TABLE: no Belgian authority publishes a code list for this domain, and no DD field bound to it is transmitted on any of the nine BE report specifications. The codes in this block are Mercans configuration storage keys, assigned on the shared/CZ_Data_Dictionary_V2_0.xlsx precedent, which carries a code on every option row. They are not authority values and must never be transmitted. |
|  |  | 2 | White-collar employee | Bediende | Holiday pay and double holiday pay paid by the employer. |
|  |  | 3 | Student | Student | Solidarity contribution for the first 650 hours per calendar year. |
|  |  | 4 | Flexi-job worker | Flexi-jobwerknemer | 28 per cent employer contribution; no employee contribution or ordinary withholding on the flexi-wage. |
|  |  | 5 | Apprentice | Leerling | Apprentice or pupil in alternating training, declared with Dimona worker type ALT and detailed by the Apprenticeship Type mapper (NSSO zone 00055). Locked in scope by scope_manifest.json option_mapper_scope.worker_status and employment_types.in_scope; added by the regression-fix pass of 16 Aug 2026, which found it missing. |
|  |  | 9 | Company director | Bedrijfsleider | Presumed SELF-EMPLOYED. Directors' fees are not employment income: the director pays quarterly contributions to a social insurance fund for the self-employed (the RSVZ / INASTI scheme), is NOT declared in the DmfA and is reported on form 281.20, not 281.10. Where a director also holds a genuine employment contract exercised under authority, only the employed function enters payroll. OUT OF SCOPE: scope_manifest.json employment_types.out_of_scope excludes company directors exercising a mandate (self-employed scheme, RSVZ/INASTI, form 281.20), and filing BE-FIN-28120-015 is descoped. The value is retained as an explicit exclusion guard, on the same convention as Dimona Worker Type codes PMP and T17, and must not be configured for a payroll population. Code 9 follows this workbook's own 'not applicable' convention. |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Option Mapper Description | Code | Abbreviation | Local Name | English Description |
|  | NSSO Worker Status | A | Artist | Artiest | Artist. NSSO worker status (statuut van de werknemer), zone 00053. Applicability: RSZ/ONSS yes, RSZPPO/ORPSS no. Quarters 1900/1 to 2003/2. Valid 01/01/1900 to 30/06/2003. Authority's remark (Dutch, verbatim): 'Het gaat om muzikanten en schouwspelartiesten, of zij nu tewerkgesteld zijn in het kader van een arbeidsovereenkomst of presteren in omstandigheden gelijkaardig aan die van een arbeidsovereenkomst, zoals gedefinieerd in artikel 3.2° van het KB van 28 november 1969.'. A code may appear more than once in this list: the NSSO splits a status into separate rows where its validity window or its RSZ / RSZPPO applicability changed, and both rows are reproduced here rather than collapsed. LANGUAGE CONVENTION FOR THIS BLOCK: the NSSO publishes this annex in Dutch and French only. Rows whose English term is not already established in this dossier carry the applicability and validity facts in English together with the authority's own Dutch wording quoted verbatim, rather than an unofficial translation, so that no cell asserts an English statutory term the NSSO has not published. Source: RSZ Bijlage 21 'Lijst van de toegelaten waarden voor het statuut van de werknemer', AN2025-1-NL21 (version 2025/1), held at 00-official-sources/dd-sources/NSSO_Annexe21_2025-1_NL.xlsx. |

Authority's remark (Dutch, verbatim): 'Het gaat om werknemers die arbeidsperiodes van beperkte duur verrichten, hetzij omwille van de seizoensgebonden aard van het werk, hetzij omdat de bedrijven die hen aanwerven verplicht zijn personeel te rekruteren ter versterking op bepaalde momenten van het jaar.'. |
|  |  | SA | Technical and administrative professional staff of the fire services | Technisch en administratief beroepspersoneel van de brandweerdiensten | Technical and administrative professional staff of the fire services. Authority's remark (Dutch, verbatim): 'Het gaat om het technisch en administratief personeel dat prestaties verricht voor de brandweerdiensten en dat opgenomen is in het specifieke personeelskader van de brandweerdiensten.'. |

|  |  | SP | Operational professional staff of the fire services | Operationeel beroepspersoneel van de brandweerdiensten | Operational professional staff of the fire services. Applicability: RSZ/ONSS no, RSZPPO/ORPSS yes. Quarters 1900/1 to 2021/4. Valid 01/01/1900 to 31/12/2021. |

|  |  | SW | Worker performing services as set out in the Law of 3 May 2024 | Werknemer die prestaties iutvoert zoals opgenomen in de wet van 3 mei 2024 | Worker performing services as set out in the Law of 3 May 2024. |  |  | T | Temporary | Tijdelijk | Temporary. Applicability: RSZ/ONSS yes, RSZPPO/ORPSS yes. Quarters 1900/1 to 9999/4. Valid 01/01/1900 to 31/12/9999. Authority's remark (Dutch, verbatim): 'Het gaat om werknemers aangeworven in het kader van een contract van bepaalde duur om te kunnen voorzien in de vervanging van een vaste werknemer of om te beantwoorden aan een tijdelijke toename van het werk of om de uitvoering van een uitzonderlijk werk te verzekeren (dit heeft geen betrekking op de uitzendkrachten die via een uitzendbureau ter beschikking worden gesteld van een werkgever).'. |
|  |  | TS | Temporary statutory staff in education, paid by a Community or by a university, a college or a local or provincial autho | Tijdelijke statutairen in het onderwijs, bezoldigd door een gemeenschap of door een universiteit, een hogeschool of een plaatselijke of provinciale overheid | Temporary statutory staff in education, paid by a Community or by a university, a college or a local or provincial authority. NSSO worker code (werknemerskengetal), zones 00037 and 00082 - the code that says which contributions are due for the worker. A code may appear more than once where the NSSO splits it by validity window or by worker type; both rows are reproduced rather than collapsed. LANGUAGE CONVENTION FOR THIS BLOCK: the NSSO publishes this annex in Dutch and French only. Rows whose English term is not already established in this dossier carry the applicability and validity facts in English together with the authority's own Dutch wording quoted verbatim, rather than an unofficial translation, so that no cell asserts an English statutory term the NSSO has not published. Source: RSZ Bijlage 2 'Lijst der werknemerskengetallen waarvoor bijdragen verschuldigd zijn', AN2024-3-NL2 (version 2024/3), held at 00-official-sources/dd-sources/NSSO_Annexe2_2024-3_NL.xlsx. |

|  |  |  |  |  | Contribution type: - Supplementaire bijdrage : stemt overeen met de supplementaire verschuldigde bijdragen voor gewone werknemers (functioneel blok bijdrage verschuldigd voor de werknemerslijn). Authority's wording (Dutch, verbatim): ''. |
|  |  |  |  |  | Contribution type: - Bijdrage niet gebonden aan een natuurlijke persoon : stemt overeen met de bijdragen niet verbonden aan een natuurlijke persoon (functioneel blok bijdrage niet gebonden aan een natuurlijke persoon). Authority's wording (Dutch, verbatim): ''. |
|  |  |  |  |  | Contribution type: - Bijzondere bijdrage aanvullende vergoedingen : stemt overeen met werknemers (werknemerslijn) die aanvullende vergoedingen krijgen waarvoor bijzondere bijdragen verschuldigd zijn (functioneel blok aanvullende vergoeding - bijdrage). Authority's wording (Dutch, verbatim): ''. |
|  |  |  |  |  | Contribution type: - Werknemersbijdrage werklozen in het stelsel werkloosheid met bedrijfstoeslag (SWT) of aanvullende vergoedingen oudere werknemers (SWAV): stemt overeen met werknemers (werknemerslijn) die aanvullende vergoedingen krijgen waarvoor bijzondere bijdragen verschuldigd zijn (functioneel blok aanvullende vergoeding - bijdrage). Authority's wording (Dutch, verbatim): ''. |
|  |  |  |  |  | Contribution type: - Bijzondere bijdrage statutaire werknemer buitenland: stemt overeen met werknemers (werknemerslijn) die in het buitenland werken. Authority's wording (Dutch, verbatim): ''. |
|  |  |  |  |  | Contribution type: - Stagiairs: stemt overeen met personen die een betaalde arbeid verrichten maar niet aan de sociale zekerheid onderworpen zijn en die niet in DmfA aangegeven zijn. Authority's wording (Dutch, verbatim): ''. |

|  | NSSO Employer Category | 000 | Algemene categorie voor werkgevers voor wie de RSZ geen speciale bijdrage voor een Fonds voor Bestaanzekerheid int en di | Algemene categorie voor werkgevers voor wie de RSZ geen speciale bijdrage voor een Fonds voor Bestaanzekerheid int en die niet opgenomen zijn in een bijzondere categorie (zie ook categorie 011). | Sector: Privé. Valid 01/01/1945 to 01/01/9999. Authority's wording (Dutch, verbatim): 'Algemene categorie voor werkgevers voor wie de RSZ geen speciale bijdrage voor een Fonds voor Bestaanzekerheid int en die niet opgenomen zijn in een bijzondere categorie (zie ook categorie 011).'. NSSO employer category (werkgeverscategorie), zones 00036 and 00019 - the three-digit index the NSSO allots to an employer, which determines the special contributions and Fonds voor Bestaanzekerheid levies due. The VALUE for a given client is still client master data taken from the NSSO registration confirmation; what this block now supplies is the published domain that value is drawn from. A code may appear more than once where it was re-used across sectors or validity windows; both rows are reproduced rather than collapsed. LANGUAGE CONVENTION FOR THIS BLOCK: the NSSO publishes this annex in Dutch and French only. Rows whose English term is not already established in this dossier carry the applicability and validity facts in English together with the authority's own Dutch wording quoted verbatim, rather than an unofficial translation, so that no cell asserts an English statutory term the NSSO has not published. Source: RSZ Bijlage 27 'Lijst der werkgeverscategorieen', AN2025-1-NL27 (version 2025/1), held at 00-official-sources/dd-sources/NSSO_Annexe27_2025-1_NL.xlsx and the companion PDF NSSO_Annexe27_werkgeverscategorieen_2025-1_NL.pdf. |

|  | Joint Committee Number | 100 | Aanvullend Paritair Comité voor de werklieden | Aanvullend Paritair Comité voor de werklieden | Category: Paritaire organen bevoegd voor de werknemers die hoofdzakelijk handarbeid verrichten en hun werkgevers. Valid 01/01/1900 to 01/01/9999. Authority's wording (Dutch, verbatim): 'Aanvullend Paritair Comité voor de werklieden'. Joint committee number (nummer van het paritair comite), zone 00046. The committee fixes the sectoral minimum scales, the index dates and the end-of-year premium that bind an employer, which is why limitation L-10 records the joint committee as the variability axis for Belgium rather than geography. Numbering: a committee is three digits (CCC), a sub-committee CCC.CC and a sub-sub-committee CCC.CC.CC, all left-aligned. LANGUAGE CONVENTION FOR THIS BLOCK: the NSSO publishes this annex in Dutch and French only. Rows whose English term is not already established in this dossier carry the applicability and validity facts in English together with the authority's own Dutch wording quoted verbatim, rather than an unofficial translation, so that no cell asserts an English statutory term the NSSO has not published. Source: RSZ Bijlage 26 'Paritaire comites', AN2023-2-NL26 (version 2023/2), held at 00-official-sources/dd-sources/NSSO_Annexe26_2023-2_NL.xlsx and the companion PDF NSSO_Annexe26_paritaire_comites_2023-2_NL.pdf. |

|  | Option Mapper Description | Code | Abbreviation | Local Name | English Description |
|  | Work Reorganisation Measure | 1 | Mesure de réduction du temps de travail dans le cadre d'un plan de redistribution du travail approuvé par le Ministre de | Mesure de réduction du temps de travail dans le cadre d'un plan de redistribution du travail approuvé par le Ministre de l'emploi et du travail | Applicability: DmfA yes, DmfAPPL no, Capelo DHG no. Quarters 2003/1 to 9999/4. Authority's wording (French, verbatim): 'Mesure de réduction du temps de travail dans le cadre d'un plan de redistribution du travail approuvé par le Ministre de l'emploi et du travail'. Work reorganisation measure (mesure de reorganisation du travail / maatregel tot reorganisatie van de arbeidstijd), zone 00051, XML label ReorganisationMeasure, functional blocks 90015 Occupation and 90438 ReorgMeasureInformation. The code says the worker is employed under a working-time reduction measure, whether or not the related contribution reduction is claimed, and it is also used where the worker performs actual work for reduced pay. The zone may not carry value 599 inside block 90438. A code may appear more than once where its DmfA and DmfAPPL validity windows differ; both rows are reproduced rather than collapsed. Legal framework: CBA no. 42 of 12 June 1987, the Law of 10 April 1995 on the redistribution of work in the public sector, and the Law of 19 July 2012 on the four-day week. LANGUAGE CONVENTION FOR THIS BLOCK: the NSSO publishes this annex in French and Dutch only. Rows whose English term is not already established in this dossier carry the applicability and validity facts in English together with the authority's own French wording quoted verbatim, rather than an unofficial translation, so that no cell asserts an English statutory term the NSSO has not published. Source: NSSO DmfA glossary, complete version, edition 2026/2 (published 01/06/2026), held at 00-official-sources/spec-sources/NSSO_DmfA_glossary_2026-2_FR.pdf, Annexe 44 'Mesures de reorganisation du travail'. |

|  | Employment Promotion Measure | 1 | travailleur engagé dans le cadre de l'A.R. 495 | travailleur engagé dans le cadre de l'A.R. 495 | Quarters 2003/1 to 2007/2. Valid 01/01/2003 to 30/06/2007. Authority's wording (French, verbatim): 'travailleur engagé dans le cadre de l'A.R. 495'. Employment promotion measure (mesure de promotion de l'emploi / maatregel ter bevordering van de werkgelegenheid), zone 00052. The first-employment-convention codes (startbaanovereenkomsten, Article 27 of the Law of 24 December 1999) take precedence over the general Royal Decree 495 code where an occupation would qualify under both. The statutory public sector is excluded from this zone. LANGUAGE CONVENTION FOR THIS BLOCK: the NSSO publishes this annex in French and Dutch only. Rows whose English term is not already established in this dossier carry the applicability and validity facts in English together with the authority's own French wording quoted verbatim, rather than an unofficial translation, so that no cell asserts an English statutory term the NSSO has not published. Source: NSSO DmfA glossary, complete version, edition 2026/2 (published 01/06/2026), held at 00-official-sources/spec-sources/NSSO_DmfA_glossary_2026-2_FR.pdf, Annexe 35 'Mesures de promotion de l'emploi'. |
|  |  | 2 | travailleur engagé dans le cadre de mesures d'activation des allocations de chômage, du revenu d'intégration ou de l'aid | travailleur engagé dans le cadre de mesures d'activation des allocations de chômage, du revenu d'intégration ou de l'aide sociale financière. Ce code doit uniquement être utilisé dans un des cas suivants: une occupation dans le cadre d'un programme de transition professionnelle reconnu, une occupation dans le cadre d'un poste de travail reconnu, une occupation dans le cadre de l'économie sociale d'insertion (SINE). | Quarters 2003/1 to 9999/4. Valid 01/01/2003 to 31/12/9999. Authority's wording (French, verbatim): 'travailleur engagé dans le cadre de mesures d'activation des allocations de chômage, du revenu d'intégration ou de l'aide sociale financière. Ce code doit uniquement être utilisé dans un des cas suivants: une occupation dans le cadre d'un programme de transition professionnelle reconnu, une occupation dans le cadre d'un poste de travail reconnu, une occupation dans le cadre de l'économie sociale d'insertion (SINE).'. |

|  | Option Mapper Description | Code | Abbreviation | Local Name | English Description |
|  | Work Schedule Days Indicator | 1 | Monday, week 1 | Maandag eerste week | Monday of week 1 of the work-schedule grid. THE GRID, NOT A CALENDAR: the days of a variable part-time schedule are declared as positions in a repeating grid (grille de travail / arbeidsrooster), functional block 90060, and the grid must have the form of exactly 1, 2, 3 or 4 weeks. A 1-week grid uses codes 1-7, a 2-week grid 1-14, a 3-week grid 1-21 and a 4-week grid 1-28; code n denotes the same weekday in every grid long enough to contain it, so one numbering serves all four grid lengths. The companion hours-per-day field is an integer in hundredths of hours, range [0;1400] (for example 7 hours 36 minutes is declared as 0760). Source: NSSO DRS/ONEM scenario 3 glossary, edition 2026/2 (published 01/06/2026), held at 00-official-sources/spec-sources/NSSO_DRS-ONEM-scenario3_2026-2_FR.pdf, Annexe 34 'Jours relatifs a la grille de travail', annex content version 2003/1, still the version carried by the 2026/2 edition. |

|  | Option Mapper Description | Code | Abbreviation | Local Name | English Description |
|  | Tax Collection District and Office | n/a | No code table exists - assigned per taxpayer | Niet beschikbaar | RESOLVED - a genuine structural absence, not a missing document. No universal code table exists because the ontvangkantoor (receiving office) is assigned by FPS Finance PER TAXPAYER at registration, on the employer's geographic location; it is not a lookup value HRBLIZZ can pre-populate. Since the January 2015 internal reorganisation that created the regional 'Teams Inning' / 'Teams Recouvrement', the old per-tax-type bureau de recette no longer exists: declaration submission still goes to the employer's own known ontvangkantoor, while case inquiries route to a regional collection centre instead. A single team per taxpayer covers income tax and VAT together, organised by arrondissement and split personne physique / personne morale; the named teams are periodically merged, renamed and relocated, which is why no stable public list is published. Assignment is by the client's registered address, exactly as for NSSO Employer Category, and the value is client master data taken from the FPS Finance registration. Sources: FPS Finance archive notice on the 2015 Teams Inning reorganisation, financien.belgium.be/nl/E-services/FinProf/Archief; SSGPI FINPROF manual 2024, held at 00-official-sources/spec-sources/SSGPI_Handleiding_FINPROF_2024_NL.pdf, confirming region-dependent receiving offices. Researched 20 August 2026, closed 16 September 2026. |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | Option Mapper Description | Code | Abbreviation | Local Name | English Description |
|  | Tax Residency Status | 1 | Resident | Rijksinwoner | Domicile or seat of wealth in Belgium (ITC 92, Article 2, paragraph 1, 1 degree). Entry in the National Register creates a rebuttable presumption of residence. Taxed on worldwide income; withholding under scale I or II with full family reductions. MERCANS-INTERNAL CODE TABLE: no Belgian authority publishes a code list for this domain, and no DD field bound to it is transmitted on any of the nine BE report specifications. The codes in this block are Mercans configuration storage keys, assigned on the shared/CZ_Data_Dictionary_V2_0.xlsx precedent, which carries a code on every option row. They are not authority values and must never be transmitted. |
|  |  | 2 | Non-resident meeting the 75 per cent condition | Niet-inwoner die aan de 75 procent-voorwaarde voldoet | Belgian professional income is at least 75 per cent of total professional income, or the person works at least 75 per cent of the contractual working time. Withholding under scale I or II with the personal and family allowances. |
|  |  | 3 | Non-resident not meeting the 75 per cent condition | Niet-inwoner die niet aan de 75 procent-voorwaarde voldoet | Withholding under scale III, which grants no personal or family allowances. A fixed federal additional levy of 7 per cent applies in place of the municipal tax. |
|  |  | 4 | Inbound taxpayer | Ingekomen belastingplichtige | Special regime since 1 January 2022, reformed with effect from income year 2025. Minimum gross remuneration of EUR 70,000 per year. |
|  |  | 5 | Inbound researcher | Ingekomen onderzoeker | Same special regime with no minimum gross remuneration condition. |
|  |  | 6 | Frontier worker under a double tax treaty | Grensarbeider onder een verdrag | Resident of a neighbouring state working in Belgium and covered by a specific treaty article. The treaty allocation of taxing rights overrides the domestic rule; social security follows Regulation (EC) 883/2004. |

|  |  | n/a | NSSO contribution payment - no ISO category purpose prescribed | RSZ-bijdragebetaling | RESOLVED, 16 September 2026 - not applicable rather than unresolved. The NSSO does not prescribe an ISO 20022 category purpose on a contribution payment: it identifies the payment by the structured communication (gestructureerde mededeling), and every remittance must carry the full company name together with either the KBO/enterprise number or the NSSO identification number. The purpose the employer states is drawn from a five-value domain of its own - bijdragen, voorschotten, bijdrageopslag, verwijlintrest, gerechtskosten - which is carried as its own mapper on this sheet under 'NSSO Contribution Payment Purpose'. No ISO code belongs in this cell, so none has been invented. Source: NSSO administrative instructions, 'Betaalinformatie', edition 2026/3. |

One further clause referencing employees partly paid in tips/service, declared without remuneration for part of their services, appears in the ONSS instructions with a possible but UNCONFIRMED 8th code; do not add a code 8 without verifying its number against the primary glossary. |

Art.29, §2, 1°, d de l'AR du 25.11.1991. | Plan de restructuration approuvé par le Ministre de l'Emploi. Art.29, §2, 1°, d de l'AR du 25.11.1991. | Authority's wording (French, verbatim): 'Plan de restructuration approuvé par le Ministre de l'Emploi. Art.29, §2, 1°, d de l'AR du 25.11.1991.'. Legal basis for part-time work with retention of benefits, zone 00096 - the statutory provision on which the company plan or the employment agreement rests. Relevant only where the enterprise-framework indicator is 1. CLOSED 16 September 2026: the earlier row on this block recorded that values beyond 4 were 'not confirmed to exist'. They do exist - the definition domain runs 1 to 10 and is printed in full in the held glossary, which had not been read for this zone. Values 5 and 6 are the Vande Lanotte defensive and offensive working-time-reduction plans, 7 the public-sector redistribution plan under the Law of 10 April 1995, and 8 to 10 the three successive CBA no. 103 time-credit regimes. LANGUAGE CONVENTION: the authority publishes this domain in French and Dutch only; the wording is quoted verbatim rather than translated, so that no cell asserts an English statutory term the NSSO has not published. Source: NSSO DRS/ONEM scenario 3 glossary, edition 2026/2 (published 01/06/2026), zone 00096 BASE LEGALE, XML label LegalBasis, functional block 90045 DeclarationPartTimeWorkWithMaintenanceRights, held at 00-official-sources/spec-sources/NSSO_DRS-ONEM-scenario3_2026-2_FR.pdf. |

|  | NSSO Contribution Payment Purpose | bijdragen | Contributions | Bijdragen | Ordinary social security contributions due on the quarter's DmfA. WHAT THE NSSO ACTUALLY REQUIRES: the NSSO does not prescribe an ISO 20022 category purpose on a contribution payment. It identifies the payment by the structured communication (gestructureerde mededeling) and by the purpose category the employer states; every remittance must also carry the full company name and either the KBO/enterprise number or the NSSO identification number. These five purpose categories are the domain. Source: NSSO administrative instructions, 'Betaalinformatie', edition 2026/3, socialsecurity.be/employer/instructions/dmfa/nl/latest/instructions/obligations/obligations_nsso/contributionpayment_obligations_periodicity/paymentmethod.html (read 16 September 2026). |

|  |  | verwijlintrest | Late-payment interest | Verwijlintrest | Interest running on a contribution paid after its due date. |
|  |  | gerechtskosten | Legal costs | Gerechtskosten | Judicial and recovery costs charged to the employer. |

|  | Risk Identification (DRS Annexe 12) | AOAT001 / 001 | AOAT001 | Scénario 1 - Déclaration d'un accident de travail Déclaration complète | Risk identification for the DRS declaration named by the form identification AOAT001. HOW THIS FIELD IS KEYED: the value actually carried on the wire in zone 00430 (IDENTIFICATION DU RISQUE, XML label IdentificationOfRisk, functional block 90064) is the three-digit code alone, numeric, length 3, permitted values 001 to 008; the form identification that gives the code its meaning travels separately in zone 00296 (Identification du formulaire, Annexe 11). The composite key used in this column is therefore the annex's own key - Identification plus Code - and not a single wire value. Sector: ACCIDENTS DE TRAVAIL. Authority's wording: 'Scénario 1 - Déclaration d'un accident de travail Déclaration complète'. Valid 01/01/1900 to 01/01/9999. Source: NSSO DRS/AMI scenario 1 glossary, edition 2026/2 (published 01/06/2026), held at 00-official-sources/spec-sources/NSSO_DRS-AMI-scenario1_2026-2_FR.pdf, Annexe 12 'Identification du risque', annex content version 2025/1 (AN2025-1-FR12). BIJLAGE 12 IS LIVE, NOT RETIRED: it is a DRS annex, not a DmfA annex, which is why the DmfA glossary's own bijlage index jumps from 11 to 21. It is carried by all three held DRS glossaries (AMI scenario 1, FAT scenario 1, ONEM scenario 3) at edition 2026/2. |

|  | Option Mapper Description | Code | Abbreviation | Local Name | English Description |
|  | DmfA Remuneration Code | 1 | Tous les montants qui sont toujours considérés comme rémunération, à l'exception des indemnités mentionnées sous un autr | Tous les montants qui sont toujours considérés comme rémunération, à l'exception des indemnités mentionnées sous un autre code. | Applicability: DmfA yes, DRS yes. Valid 01/01/1900 to 01/01/9999. Authority's wording (French, verbatim): 'Tous les montants qui sont toujours considérés comme rémunération, à l'exception des indemnités mentionnées sous un autre code.'. DmfA remuneration code (code de remuneration / bezoldigingscode), zone 00067 - the code under which a pay element's gross amount is declared on the DmfA, and therefore the join between this Data Dictionary and every wage type in BE-WTC-001. Code 1 is the residual: every amount always treated as remuneration except those listed under another code. Codes 61 to 67 mirror codes 1, 2, 5 and 51 for amounts paid to definitively appointed public-sector staff that are EXEMPT from the public-sector pension contribution, and apply only to full public-sector statutaires. LANGUAGE CONVENTION FOR THIS BLOCK: the NSSO publishes this annex in French and Dutch only. Rows whose English term is not already established in this dossier carry the applicability and validity facts in English together with the authority's own French wording quoted verbatim, rather than an unofficial translation, so that no cell asserts an English statutory term the NSSO has not published. Source: NSSO DmfA glossary, complete version, edition 2026/2 (published 01/06/2026), held at 00-official-sources/spec-sources/NSSO_DmfA_glossary_2026-2_FR.pdf, Annexe 7 'Codes de remuneration'. |
|  |  | 2 | Les primes et les avantages similaires accordés indépendamment du nombre de journées de travail prestées effectivement d | Les primes et les avantages similaires accordés indépendamment du nombre de journées de travail prestées effectivement durant le trimestre de la déclaration. | Applicability: DmfA yes, DRS yes. Valid 01/01/1900 to 01/01/9999. Valid 01/01/2007 to 01/01/9999. Authority's wording (French, verbatim): 'Pécule simple de vacances de sortie payé aux employés et non soumis aux cotisations.'. |
|  |  | 12 | Partie du pécule simple de vacances qui correspond au salaire normal des jours de vacances et qui a été payé anticipativ | Partie du pécule simple de vacances qui correspond au salaire normal des jours de vacances et qui a été payé anticipativement par l'employeur précédent et non soumis aux cotisations. A partir du premier trimestre 2024, il s'agit de 90% du salaire brut par jour de congé pris et, le cas échéant, du montant qui lors du décompte final du pécule de vacances peut être déduit du montant du pécule simple de vacances effectivement dû par le nouvel employeur. | Applicability: DmfA yes, DRS yes. Valid 01/01/2007 to 01/01/9999. Authority's wording (French, verbatim): 'Partie du pécule simple de vacances qui correspond au salaire normal des jours de vacances et qui a été payé anticipativement par l'employeur précédent et non soumis aux cotisations. A partir du premier trimestre 2024, il s'agit de 90% du salaire brut par jour de congé pris et, le cas échéant, du montant qui lors du décompte final du pécule de vacances peut être déduit du montant du pécule simple de vacances effectivement dû par le nouvel employeur.'. |

|  |  | 14 | Montant qui lors du décompte annuel du pécule simple de vacances doit être payé complémentairement si le montant avancé | Montant qui lors du décompte annuel du pécule simple de vacances doit être payé complémentairement si le montant avancé par le nouvel employeur ne suffit pas et qu'une partie du pécule simple de vacances doit encore être payée. | Applicability: DmfA yes, DRS yes. |  | Option Mapper Description | Code | Abbreviation | Local Name | English Description |
|  | DmfA Service Code | 1 | toutes les données relatives au temps de travail couvertes par une rémunération avec cotisations ONSS, à l'exception des | toutes les données relatives au temps de travail couvertes par une rémunération avec cotisations ONSS, à l'exception des vacances légales et complémentaires des ouvriers | Applicability: DmfA yes, DmfAPPL yes. Quarters 2003/1 to 9999/4. Valid 01/01/1900 to 01/01/9999. Authority's wording (French, verbatim): 'toutes les données relatives au temps de travail couvertes par une rémunération avec cotisations ONSS, à l'exception des vacances légales et complémentaires des ouvriers'. DmfA service code (code prestation / prestatiecode), zone 00062 - the code under which days and hours of working time are declared on the DmfA. Code 1 is the residual for all working time covered by pay carrying NSSO contributions, except statutory and supplementary holiday for blue-collar workers. The indicative codes in the 50 to 76 range cover time the employer does not pay and another institution authenticates - illness, work accident, occupational disease, maternity and the temporary unemployment schemes - and are declared for entitlement and holiday-accrual purposes rather than for contribution calculation. LANGUAGE CONVENTION FOR THIS BLOCK: the NSSO publishes this annex in French and Dutch only. Rows whose English term is not already established in this dossier carry the applicability and validity facts in English together with the authority's own French wording quoted verbatim, rather than an unofficial translation, so that no cell asserts an English statutory term the NSSO has not published. Source: NSSO DmfA glossary, complete version, edition 2026/2 (published 01/06/2026), held at 00-official-sources/spec-sources/NSSO_DmfA_glossary_2026-2_FR.pdf, Annexe 8 'Codes prestation'. |
